# Repo reorg report — 2026-09-30

- **Prompt:** `prompts/05_repo_cleanup.md` (Repo cleanup), run in Claude Code on branch `claude/charming-lamport-it1dac`.
- **Scope:** Steps 0–6 of that prompt, with the answers given during the run (section 2). Repo-work notes; tags follow AGENTS.md (Verified = read in the file or the git history; Inferred = derived, reasoning stated).
- **Basis:** checkout `92585e6` (792 tracked files, flat root) and `origin/main` `9fec0a2` after `git fetch`.

## 1. What happened

- **Step 0.** The unauthenticated GET returned 200 (`"visibility": "public"`). Carl answered "public repo is fine" and later "The repo stays public." (section 2). Verified.
- **Step 1.** Mapping table for the 792 files at `92585e6`, each placed from its own content (lane field, Ledger file named, citations) by script and by six independent verifier/skeptic agent pairs. 0 lane disputes. Verified.
- **Before Step 2, `git fetch`** showed 31 new commits on `origin/main`: 6 uploads by Carl (6 source PDFs in a root `Library/` folder, 2 Bluebeam OCR copies) and 23 commits by a second Claude Code session, branch `claude/sharp-thompson-5q5d1f`, merged as PRs #1 and #2. That session had already applied the moves: 752 of 792 files sat at the paths approved here, blob IDs unchanged. The other 40 are the byte-identical Contract & General page duplicate, parked one folder deeper than proposed; Carl chose to leave it (section 2). Verified. The overlap is logged in ISSUES_LOG.md.
- **This session** therefore made no moves. It fast-forwarded to `9fec0a2` and added only what was still open: the three lane package READMEs, this prompt, the README pointer and Folders section, the library manifest and plan-set crosswalk, this report, the log entries and the Step 6 rulebook edits.

## 2. Answers and decisions given during the run

- **Step 0 (visibility):** "public repo is fine"
- **A. Branch:** "Use claude/charming-lamport-it1dac. This session can only push there."
- **B–E:** "As recommended." (B: download → .gitattributes, download (1) → .gitignore; C: rebuilt Civil_and_Site_Components/ and Components/ parents; D: flatten the C&G bundle, identical duplicate to _unsorted/; E: restore the three lane READMEs from history)
- **F. Root README:** "Make README (2).md the root README instead of the Step 4 rebuild. Add one line pointing to testbeds/eastsound/."
- **G. This prompt:** "Save the prompt as prompts/05_repo_cleanup.md."
- **Conflict 1:** "Test bed tools go to testbeds/eastsound/tools/, per AGENTS.md."
- **Conflict 2:** "Library_Fingerprint*.csv go to testbeds/eastsound/process/, per AGENTS.md."
- **Conflict 3:** "library/ holds source documents plus .md notes beside them. AGENTS rule 2 governs; my \"source documents only\" line was wrong."
- **Conflict 4:** "graph/ vs graphify-out/, transfer/, the separate-repo question, where the Missing_Pages PNGs go, Library_Manifest.csv: no files exist for these yet. List them as open decisions in the reorg report; don't create folders for them."
- **Step 6 additions:** "Prompt 1: remove the private-repo stop and \"commit nothing while it's public.\" Record in DECISIONS.md, decided by Carl: \"The repo stays public.\"" · "AGENTS rule 5: allow .docx only as a reading copy in process/." · "AGENTS layout: derived/ is owned by the lane that writes each subfolder; _unsorted/ is owned by Carl."
- **Other session:** "sharp-thompson is closed. This session is the only one working these folders from here."
- **C&G duplicate:** "Leave the Contract & General duplicate where it is."
- **Test-bed README:** "Keep the kit's text in testbeds/eastsound/README.md and append a short \"Folders\" section: one line per folder as it actually exists on main."
- **derived/ wording:** "Yes, use that wording for derived/." ("each subfolder is owned by the lane that writes it; bluebeam-ocr/ is owned by Carl")
- **Prompt 1 decision list:** "Yes, replace \"Repo is private\" with \"The repo stays public.\""
- **Library drawing set:** "The three native parts are the library's drawing set. Don't wait for the 26 plans_N extracts."
- **Division 26 extract:** "Division 26 extract not uploaded; main spec governs."
- **Fingerprints:** "Don't edit Library_Fingerprint.csv; it records the Claude Project's converted copies."

## 7. Library findings

- **Native files (Verified, `index/Library_Manifest.csv`):** 6 PDFs. The main spec (645 pp.), Add. 4 (13 pp.) and the CQA plan (23 pp.) match 00 Register rows 1, 3 and 4 by name and page count. The three plan-set parts (27 + 21 + 48 pp.) carry set pp.1–96 and have no Register rows. Page counts read with pypdf 5.1.0 and PyMuPDF 1.28.2 in a scratch environment (not in `requirements.txt`, not committed); the two agree on every file. Source URL: not stated anywhere in the repo.
- **Crosswalk (Verified / Verified-Visual, `index/Plan_Set_Crosswalk.csv`):** 99 rows, 89 plans_N pages from the 01 page log plus the 10 sheets with no plans_N extract. All 96 set pages covered. Each row checked against the native title block: 90 in the text layer, 9 image-only pages (set pp.28, 29, 45, 56–61) from the rendered title block. 0 mismatches. It also agrees row for row with `library/Plan_Set_Parts.md`.
- **S-sheet page total:** the native S1.1–S4.1 title blocks read "56 OF 96" to "61 OF 96" (Verified-Visual at 300 dpi). 01 item 10 logged "OF 98" at the stored image resolution. For Setup's 01 rev2.
- **Fingerprint mismatch, expected:** the native files' SHA-256 values differ from `process/Library_Fingerprint.csv`, which records the Claude Project's converted copies (page bundles and text-only files; for example the main spec is 1,436,556 bytes there, 17,502,025 bytes native). Not edited. Graph_Transfer_Ultraplan §6 Step 4 (text must match on all 888 pages) needs revising before a Transfer run.
- **Division 26 extract:** not uploaded; the main spec governs (decision).

## 9. Other findings

- **Two sessions ran the same step.** The `claude/sharp-thompson-5q5d1f` session moved the tree while this session was waiting at the Step 1 approval stop. The results agreed, but the rule is one build session at a time (AGENTS rule 6). Logged in ISSUES_LOG.md.
- **Empty upload commit** `9a39feb` ("Add files via upload") adds nothing; its tree equals its parent's. Which files it was meant to add is not stated.
- **Civil & Site as-delivered notes predate the 2026-09-29 resplit** (the six added rows have no note; the 23 updated rows show pre-resplit sheets), though both Civil READMEs are dated Sep 30. The Project Ledger governs.
- **"Owning lane" / "Owner lane"** in Contract & General and Structural & Building notes name the design owner, not the lane that wrote the file. Files were placed by the lane that wrote them (the Ledger file each names).
- **Duplicate Tags in `Project_Ledger.csv`:** SD-1 (Civil storm drain vs E&C sludge pump; Merge Issue #3) and PROPOSED-Influent-Sampler (Exception #13). `Project_Ledger_by_CWP.csv` has 19 columns but matches `*Ledger*.csv`. Prompt 1's ledger check will need baseline exception rows for both.
- **Names in a public repo:** commit author metadata uses a big-d.com address, and "Carl Schaefbauer" appears in both plans. Carl decided the repo stays public; noted once against AGENTS rule 1.
- **Windows paths:** the longest path is 191 characters (E&C `Electrical_and_Controls_Page_Components/Drawings/073 E5.1 …`). A clone root longer than about 68 characters exceeds MAX_PATH without `core.longpaths`.
- **The Merge Issues Log** (`project/03_Exceptions_and_Issues/Issues_Log.md`) says status changes are edited in place, which conflicts with AGENTS rule 4; the checked append-only rule covers only the three root logs.
